2026-10-22T03:05:06.0271008Z   UV_CACHE_DIR: /home/runner/work/_temp/setup-uv-cache
2026-10-22T03:05:06.0283026Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Serve the Decode step reductions B1, B4, B5 and B6 on top of the 2-warpgroup MegaMoE and hot-4 replication (image 39711569005)

Why: the score is Decode-bound. Decode-0 ran at its 32-request cap 61% of
the time (39622940739), and raising the cap past 128 rows hit tile cliffs
in three kernels (39699383079). A Decode step is about 31.8 ms per rank
(39550627795 decode-0 profile):
- MegaMoE SwapAB 18.7 ms;
- FA3 verify 4.8 ms;
- dense FP8 QKV/O 3.0 ms;
- everything else 5.3 ms.
One ms off the step is worth about +3.2%. Four Codex workstreams prepared
independent reductions. They are combined here so that one image build
(39711569005) and one serving run cover all of them.

What, on top of 531c78a (2-warpgroup Prefill MegaMoE, hot-4 expert
replication, SwapAB-144 host) with the Decode cap back at 128, c192 and
graph sizes ending at 32:
- B1 (patches/deep-gemm-overlay/sm90-decode-swapab-prefetch.patch,
  appended to deep_gemm_overlay_patches): in the Decode dynamic scheduler
  the A loader claims and publishes the next block before processing the
  current block's activations. The weight loader then waits less at block
  boundaries. Decode SwapAB 168 registers, 0 spill. Prefill and non-swap
  SASS are unchanged. Expected 0-3 us per layer.
- B4 (patches/sglang/decode-verify-scheduler-metadata.patch): TARGET_VERIFY
  builds the FA3 scheduler metadata once per step and shares it across the
  94 layers, inside CUDA graphs too. It is limited to B32..36, q4, D128,
  FP8, page 64. Expected about 0.06-0.14 ms per step.
  patches/sglang/verify-attention-candidates.patch only adds the helper
  module that the new gate uses; it changes no serving path. Alternative
  verify kernels (FlashInfer FA2/FA3, decode x4, Triton) were estimated
  slower from their sources and are not used.
- B5 (host patch sm90-1d2d-small-m.patch, built into 39711569005 by
  3f98e64): with DG_SM90_1D2D_SMALL_M=1, exported for Decode workers only,
  DeepGEMM's dense FP8 1d2d GEMM uses a single M tile for 65-144 rows
  (BLOCK_M 256, BLOCK_N 80 for QKV N9216/K4096, BLOCK_N 32 for O
  N4096/K8192, one wave). The stock layout is two 64-row M tiles. The
  weights are read once instead of twice. Scales, FP8 operands and FP32
  accumulation are unchanged. DeepGEMM's own cost model predicts it slower
  because of the 50% M padding. This run decides it from the decode
  profile's 1d2d kernel time (stock 17.3 / 14.3 us).
- B6 (patches/sglang/decode-fusions.patch): QK-norm/RoPE head-parallel
  launch, and the Triton router fused with the zero-bias fill and the
  padding mask. Expected 0.5-0.8 ms per step.
- sglang.sbatch runs scripts/check-firmus-decode-kernels.py as a second
  numeric gate before model startup, after the MegaMoE gate. It checks the
  verify metadata sharing (warmup, capture, changed lengths and page
  tables, 94 calls, reversed bucket replay), the B6 fusions and both dense
  host layouts against FP32 references and in changed-input graphs.
  Tolerances are unchanged.
- Bench-only material (patches/sglang-bench, bench script cases) is
  included for later diagnosis. Two tests that tied the bench layout to
  the serving overlay order were dropped, because the serving overlays now
  include those patches.

Precision: no new quantization or approximation. EAGLE3's draft stays BF16.
Every change only alters scheduling, tiling or kernel fusion.

Expected: Decode step -0.6 to -1.2 ms without B5, i.e. +2-4% (about
8500-8700). B5 adds or subtracts up to about 1 ms. The decode profile's
per-kernel times attribute each change.
